# MS.990 Parallel Edition: Canvases 455–484

## Page 510 — IIIF Canvas #455

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0455.JP2` (5735 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0455.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0455.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,380

Continuation of the defense of sympathetic remedies (citing the Bishop of Lavant, Stadion, and Parisian theologians), followed by a section on the transplantation of diseases into animals and trees, illustrated with a watercolor scene of a dog eating from a tub and a man working on a tree.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Transplantation of disease into a dog and a tree** (`#xywh=0,0,5735,6853`): Colored watercolor and ink illustration in the middle of the right column. At the upper left is a yellow sun with a face and stylized blue-grey clouds. On the left, a brown dog eats from a wooden tub/bucket. On the right, a man in a yellow coat and red breeches stands before a large green deciduous tree, boring or plugging a hole in its trunk. A blue horizontal line borders the bottom of the scene.

## Page 511 — IIIF Canvas #456

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0456.JP2` (6014 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0456.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0456.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,360

Reflections on accepting gout as God's penance rather than transferring it to a faithful dog, followed by sympathetic tree-transplantation cures for limb atrophy and childhood hernias (passing a naked child through a split sapling at new moon), and the beginning of a new section 'Anatomia der Wundt Kreidter' with an illustration of four flowering wound-herbs.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Four flowering wound-herbs (Wundkräuter)** (`#xywh=0,0,6014,6853`): Colored watercolor and ink illustration in the middle of the right column above the rubricated heading 'Anatomia der Wundt Kreidter.' Four flowering herbs grow side by side in brown soil, each with green stems and leaves: from left to right, pale blue/white flowers, yellow flowers, red flowers, and yellow-green/amber flowers.

## Page 51 / 12 (fol. 226r) — IIIF Canvas #457

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0457.JP2` (5732 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0457.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0457.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

Continuation of an alchemical/medical preparation describing the three principles (salt, spirit, and earth/caput mortuum) and herbal extracts; followed by rubricated recipes 'Wie Man auß ein Jeden khraudt khan ein magisterium machen' (How to make a magisterium from any herb) and '☉ Cranij humani Zu machen' (To make ☉ of human cranium).

## Page 50 / 13 (fol. 226v) — IIIF Canvas #458

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0458.JP2` (6040 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0458.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0458.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,680

Continuation of the human cranium preparation; followed by rubricated recipes 'Von krebs augen' (On crabs' eyes), 'Magisterium Von kreps Augen' (Magisterium of crabs' eyes), 'Ciderani. ▽.' (Ciderani water), 'Hundt Leben Machen Nim Grosse vnd Wunnderliche Wirckhung' (accompanied by an illustration of a fluted glass flask), and 'Guldtne Milch' (Golden milk).

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Fluted glass flask (right column, beside 'Hundt Leben Machen')** (`#xywh=0,0,6040,6853`): A hand-drawn and painted illustration positioned on the left side of the right column alongside the rubricated heading 'Hundt Leben Machen Nim Grosse vnd Wunnderliche Wirckhung'. It depicts a round-bottomed, vertically ribbed/fluted glass flask or phial with a narrow neck and flared lip, shaded in blue-grey wash to indicate glass transparency, resting on a tan/ochre rectangular table or stand with a cast shadow.

## Page 515 — IIIF Canvas #460

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0460.JP2` (6054 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0460.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0460.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,150

Alchemical and chemical recipes: continuation of a recipe with mace and cloves; silver-plating brass ('Mößing Zu VerSilbern'); cosmetic/medicinal 'Maill Khugl' with two drawn spheres; another ball recipe; making a bone/ivory ('Ein Pain Zu Machen'); a stone/bone that always gives fire ('Pain der Aderzeit feuer gibt'); distilling oil from a brick ('Oll auß einen Zieg Zu brinen'); making water from salt and sal ammoniac in a black radish ('Waßer auß ⊖ ✶ Zu machen'); making a pleasant scent in a chimney/room; and 'Vom Pain Essenz' (bone essence) for spleen, liver obstructions, and constipation.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Maill Khugl** (`#xywh=0,0,6054,6853`): Two small shaded circular drawings of round balls or spheres (possibly medicinal or cosmetic soap balls) placed in the left column directly to the left of the rubricated heading 'Maill Khugl'.

## Page 516 — IIIF Canvas #461

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0461.JP2` (5712 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0461.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0461.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,750

Medical treatise on joint disease and gout ('Von der Glider Sucht. Arthritis'), featuring a large colored illustration of a man on crutches and a woman offering a cup. Discusses persistent vs. wandering gout (Podagra in the feet, Chiragra in the hands), accompanying fever, loss of appetite, and the role of a sharp sour salt humor obstructing nourishment and gnawing at the periosteum and nerves.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Von der Glider Sucht. Arthritis** (`#xywh=0,0,5712,6853`): Colored pen-and-ink and watercolor illustration in the upper left column depicting a male patient suffering from gout/arthritis and a female attendant. On the left, a bearded man wearing a green-and-yellow turban-like cap and a long reddish-brown belted coat walks hunched forward on two wooden crutches, looking toward the right. On the right, a woman in a blue-and-red bodice with a plumed headdress stands behind a green upholstered high-backed chair, extending a small dark cup or goblet in her right hand toward the man. Below the illustration is the rubricated title 'Von der Glider Sucht. Arthritis'.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |

| 2 | Arthritis | Arthritis *— Note: Rubricated subheading in red ink* |

## Page 517 — IIIF Canvas #462

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0462.JP2` (6091 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0462.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0462.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,100

Continuation of the treatise on arthritis/gout: distinguishing Podagra from true arthritis; the causes of the disease ('Die Vrsach diser khranckheit sind Vnderschidlich'), including overeating/overdrinking, anger, strong wine, sharp brain fluxes, heavy lifting, and chilling; prognosis (generally not fatal and often associated with longevity, though dangerous in weak old age, potentially leading to fever, pleurisy, stroke, or palpitations); and treatment via diet, abstention from wine and sour foods, purging/evacuation, and prescribed pills followed by mineral salt and oil.

## Page 518 — IIIF Canvas #463

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0463.JP2` (5732 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0463.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0463.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Continuation of recipes and humoral theory regarding podagra (gout) and the use of hermodactili (autumn crocus) with purgatives, followed by a rubricated secretum for arthritic/gouty patients (an infusion of bryony, hermodactili, mechoacanna, China root, agaric, rhubarb, sandalwood, myrobalans, sassafras, coriander, juniper, chicory, and tartar), a soothing limb salve, red-ink notes on blood purification, and a germander-must cure.

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0464.JP2` (6073 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0464.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0464.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,400

Recipes for external plasters, internal purgations (with mercurius dulcis, hermodactili, jalap, scammony, agaric, and extracts) for gout and joint pains, external washes, oils (oleum cerae, tobacco oil, earthworm oil), a burnt earthworm powder for wounds and dropsy, and a rubricated section on Arthritis Vaga (wandering gout).

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0465.JP2` (5708 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0465.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0465.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Continuation of the treatment of Arthritis Vaga (wandering gout), covering laxatives, bloodletting and inspecting the blood, absorbent powders, a famous gout powder, crystal powder, an herbal broth drink, dietary rules (avoiding sour, salted, and heavy foods), and an external compress with spirit of earthworms, sal ammoniac, scurvy-grass, Venetian soap, opium, and saffron.

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0466.JP2` (6041 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0466.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0466.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,450

Anatomical illustration of the human spinal cord (with 30 numbered vertebrae) and branching nervous system, followed by a rubricated section on the origin and diseases of the nerves (47 nerves from brain and spinal cord, 60 from the spine, spiritus animales, apoplexy, contractures, and injuries from cold, vapors, and dryness).

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**** (`#xywh=400,400,2320,1000`): Anatomical illustration in blue wash and black/brown ink occupying the upper two-thirds of the left column, depicting a standing human figure on a green-brown grassy mound above a double blue horizontal border. The central spinal column is drawn with 30 individually numbered vertebrae (1–30), from which blue branching nerves radiate outward into the head, arms, hands, torso, legs, and feet.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0467.JP2` (5711 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0467.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0467.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,850

Continuation on nerve injuries from sharp humors and gout, followed by recipes for nerve oils, ointments, and fomentations (in cold and hot causes), and a rubricated section on nerves bruised or injured by a knife (distinguishing cut, slit, and punctured nerves).

## Page 232 — IIIF Canvas #469

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0469.JP2` (5646 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0469.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0469.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,650

Introduction to the chapter on gout (Podagra) and its remedies, featuring an illustration of a seated man with gouty feet resting on a pillow alongside a verse on patient suffering, followed by medical theory on hot and cold gout, a rubricated note on prevention, and several compound recipes including Electuario Caricostino and purgative pills.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Für dein Kranckheit ist khein Khraudt...** (`#xywh=400,400,2123,1000`): Colored ink and wash illustration of a bearded man suffering from gout (Podagra) seated in a wooden armchair. He wears a red fur-trimmed coat, a red cap with grey fur trim, and mustard-yellow boots/stockings, with both feet resting on a round white-grey cushion on the ground. He holds a long wooden staff or crutch horizontally across his lap with both hands.

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0470.JP2` (6046 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0470.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0470.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,720

Continuation of gout remedies, including herbal footbaths for strengthening weak gouty feet (illustrated with a figure soaking feet in a wooden tub), an earthworm oil secretum, an apothecary bottle illustration with instructions for applying the oil, and a 'miraculous remedy' using cat fat and vinegar.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Ein khöstliches Badt für die Podagraische schwache füeß.** (`#xywh=400,400,2323,1000`): Colored ink and wash illustration in the lower left column depicting a person's bare lower legs and feet (from the thighs/knees down) soaking in a round, hooped wooden tub filled with water.

**Die Artzeney wird also gebraucht.** (`#xywh=400,400,2323,1000`): Colored ink and wash illustration in the upper-middle right column depicting a rectangular blue-grey apothecary glass bottle with a narrow neck and flared lip, resting on a yellow-brown circular base.

## Page 527 — IIIF Canvas #471

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0471.JP2` (5713 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0471.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0471.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,920

Page 527 (fol. 233) containing a medicinal wine and several purgative powders for gout, followed by the famous recipe for Queen Elizabeth of Hungary's Water (Aqua Reginae Hungariae), illustrated with a golden royal crown and a small red apothecary jar.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Volgen Andere der gleichen, Khöstliche Pulluer für die Podagraische** (`#xywh=400,400,2156,1000`): Small colored illustration on the left margin/border of the left column depicting a small red cylindrical apothecary jar or capsule with a dark grey/blue rim at top and bottom.

**Der Khünigin Elisabeth in Ungern Von Gott geoffen bahrtes ▽ für das Podagra** (`#xywh=400,400,2156,1000`): Colored ink and wash illustration in the middle of the right column depicting a golden royal crown with a red velvet cap/lining and jeweled/ornamented rim, resting on a yellow-brown base.

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0472.JP2` (6013 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0472.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0472.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

Early New High German medical recipes for purgatives (using scammony, turpeth, mechoacana, hermodactyl, black hellebore, and diaphoretic antimony), pain-relieving poultices (woman's milk, rose water, egg yolk, cow's milk), cleansing powders, and a detailed herbal bath and wine infusion regimen.

## Page 529 — IIIF Canvas #473

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0473.JP2` (5707 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0473.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0473.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,700

Continuation of the wine regimen, a precious water for podagra in the feet, and a major illustrated treatise 'Von dem Schiatica' (Of Sciatica) describing its humoral pathology, three diagnostic signs, sudorific treatments (sassafras, lignum sanctum, china root, sarsaparilla), and a pitch-plaster cure.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Illustration of a man suffering from sciatica (Von dem Schiatica)** (`#xywh=400,400,2153,1000`): A hand-colored ink and watercolor illustration positioned on the left side of the left column next to the heading 'Von dem Schiatica'. It depicts a bearded man in profile facing left, hunched forward in pain and leaning heavily on a wooden walking stick or crutch held in his right hand. He wears a yellow-ochre doublet/jacket with rolled sleeves, dark brown breeches, tan stockings, and brown shoes, standing on a patch of green grass.

## Page 530 — IIIF Canvas #474

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0474.JP2` (6004 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0474.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0474.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,700

Clysters, plasters, cerates, and ointments for sciatica, podagra, and limb pains, including recipes with cowslip, betony, bryony root, dwarf elder juice, pine resin, ship's pitch, galbanum, wormwood juice, ox gall, rectified mercury, and green pomegranates.

## Page 531 — IIIF Canvas #475

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0475.JP2` (5710 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0475.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0475.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

Recipes for sciatica (including internal infusions, an artificial bath, and electuaries/ointments), a wound-healing plaster, a secretum for sciatica and gout, a remedy for swollen feet from podagra (illustrated with a watercolor of a foot in a wooden tub), and an aqua recipe.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Foot in a wooden tub (podagra footbath)** (`#xywh=400,400,2155,1000`): Watercolor illustration in the lower right column beside the rubricated heading 'Ein Köstliches Mitel für Groß Geschwollne füeß Von Podagra'. It depicts a bare lower leg and foot (flesh-toned) immersed in blue-tinted water inside a brown hooped wooden tub or bucket resting on a yellowish-brown round base.

## Page 532 — IIIF Canvas #476

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0476.JP2` (6013 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0476.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0476.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,550

Treatise on erysipelas ('Von der Schön' / St. Anthony's fire), illustrated with a watercolor of a seated man with inflamed, swollen lower legs, followed by internal and external remedies, fumigant powders, and poultices ('Umb Schlag').

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Seated man with erysipelas ('Die Schön') on his lower legs** (`#xywh=400,400,2306,1000`): Watercolor illustration in the upper left column beside the rubricated heading 'Von der Schön'. It depicts a seated man in profile facing right, wearing a white/pinkish shirt and yellowish-brown breeches, sitting on a grey stone block. His exposed lower legs and feet are markedly swollen, inflamed, and bright reddish-orange, illustrating erysipelas.

## Page 533 — IIIF Canvas #477

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0477.JP2` (5749 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0477.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0477.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,550

Continuation of poultices, ointments, and powders for erysipelas ('Ein Sälbel Zur Schön Und Hitzigen Schäden', 'Volgentes Pulvier Ist ein fertiges Schatz für die Schön'), followed by a treatise on knee swellings ('Von Khine Schwaam') illustrated with a watercolor of a swollen knee, and remedies for mixed apostemes.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Swollen knee ('Khine Schwaam')** (`#xywh=400,400,2174,1000`): Watercolor illustration in the lower left column beside the rubricated heading 'Von Khine Schwaam.' and the pencil foliation '236'. It depicts a bare bent leg (flesh-toned) resting on a patch of pale green/yellowish ground, showing a prominent, inflamed, reddish-brown swelling on the knee joint.

## Page 533 — IIIF Canvas #478

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0478.JP2` (6013 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0478.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0478.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

Recipes for cold swellings of the knees (plasters and cataplasms to resolve, dry, and soften hard swellings), followed by surgical instructions for incising knee-water ('Wie die Khnie Wasser am Best Geschniden Werd'), accompanied by an illustration of a seated man with a swollen knee.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**** (`#xywh=400,400,2306,1000`): Color wash illustration in the lower-left quadrant showing a man in a pinkish-red tunic seated on a rectangular stone block, facing right in three-quarter profile with hands clasped over his chest and bare legs/feet resting on a dark greenish-brown sloped ground. His forward (right) knee appears slightly swollen. Framed by a thin red-ink border on top and bottom, a thick red-ink vertical band separating it from the right-hand text column, and an empty rectangular label box below.

## Page 534 — IIIF Canvas #479

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0479.JP2` (5668 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0479.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0479.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,750

Continuation of post-incision wound care for knee-water, followed by remedies for joint-water ('glid Wasser') and a section on swollen feet ('Volgt Weider Von den geschwollnen fiesen') featuring vapor-bath and footbath recipes with two illustrations.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**** (`#xywh=400,400,2134,1000`): Color wash illustration in the upper-middle of the right column showing a white cloth draped over a shallow three-legged pan or brazier filled with glowing red coals/embers, resting on a yellow-ochre surface, illustrating a vapor/fumigation treatment for swollen feet.

**** (`#xywh=400,400,2134,1000`): Color wash illustration in the lower-left of the right column showing a round wooden stave footbath tub bound with three horizontal hoops and filled with grey-blue liquid, resting on a yellow-ochre surface.

## Page 535 — IIIF Canvas #480

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0480.JP2` (6055 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0480.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0480.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,650

Footbath recipes for gouty and swollen feet, a clinical discussion distinguishing hot vs. cold humors in foot swellings ('Von Underschidt der geschwulst an fiesen'), a cataplasm and plaster recipe, and a section on watery swellings ('Tumor aquosus. Wäßerige geschwulst').

## Page 536 — IIIF Canvas #481

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0481.JP2` (5762 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0481.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0481.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

Surgical and medical recipes for flatulent tumors (Tumor flatulentus) from winds and fluxes, and for cold, phlegmatic tumors on the feet, knees, and arms, including herbal poultice bags, a watercolor illustration of a bent leg, cataplasms, ointments (Unguentum Agrippa), cherry-gum plasters, and internal/external decoctions.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Cold tumor on the leg and foot (in 'Von denen khalten Tumoren an denen fießen vnd armen')** (`#xywh=400,400,2181,1000`): Colored ink and watercolor illustration placed in the lower-middle portion of the left column, showing a bare human right leg bent at the knee, resting on a green grassy mound. The leg faces left, illustrating cold, phlegmatic swelling around the knee, calf, and foot.

## Page 537 — IIIF Canvas #482

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0482.JP2` (6037 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0482.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0482.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Continuation of poultices and plasters for cold swellings (including Emplastrum de Cumino, Emplastrum de Baccis Lauri, and cataplasms with mercury and bryony), followed by a section on old ulcers and sores on the feet ('Von alle Schäden an fießen'), accompanied by an illustration of a seated man with ulcerated legs.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Seated man with ulcerated legs (above 'Von alle Schäden an fießen')** (`#xywh=400,400,2318,1000`): Colored ink and watercolor illustration at the top of the right column depicting a man seated on a wooden chest/bench inside a room with a paned window and a small wooden table. He wears a pink/reddish tunic and yellow breeches, bending forward to hold and examine his bare swollen lower leg and foot, which display red sores/ulcers on the shin, ankle, and top of the foot.

## Page 538 — IIIF Canvas #483

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0483.JP2` (5711 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0483.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0483.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,300

Continuation of remedies for old, inflamed, and ulcerated foot sores, including cooling waters, Emplastrum Griseum, a wound drink ('Volgentes Wundt Tranckh', numbered 239 in red ink), a healing bath for stubborn old sores, 'Bälbel St. Jax' ointment, a lead-and-calamine plaster, a cooling liniment, an unguent, and 'Bälbel So fleiß Macht'.

## Page 539 — IIIF Canvas #484

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0484.JP2` (6040 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0484.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0484.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,650

Surgical recipes and instructions for testing and dulcifying mercury precipitate, preparing lead-mercury ointments and wound salves, an incarnative wound powder, a note on internal purgation and antimony-sarsaparilla decoction for shinbone ulcers, and a cleansing powder made with orpiment.
